Packet 7 (Physics) of ScibowlDB HW:Back to all sets

Source packet

1. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice In ferromagnetic materials such as in hard drives, many adjacent atoms or molecules can associate and stay magnetized in the same direction. The unit that acts as a bit in a hard drive is called a
W) magnetic group
X) magnetic cluster
Y) magnetic domain
Z) magnetic block
ANSWER: Y) magnetic domain
2. BONUS Physics Short Answer In the hard drives of the 2000s, the probe that reads whether a bit is registered as a 1 or a 0 depends upon dramatically changing resistance in the presence of magnetic fields. What is this effect called?
ANSWER: GIANT MAGNETORESISTANCE
3. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer Neutrons are an example of what type of hadron?
ANSWER: BARYONS (also accept: BARYON)
4. BONUS Physics Short Answer In particle physics, what does the acronym QCD stand for?
ANSWER: QUANTUM CHROMODYNAMICS
5. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice If you mix equal amounts of red and blue light, what color is produced?
W) white
X) yellow
Y) magenta
Z) cyan
ANSWER: Y) magenta
6. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice If you mix equal amounts of cyan and yellow paint, what color of paint is produced?
W) white
X) red
Y) blue
Z) green
ANSWER: Z) green
7. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice The force of air resistance is related to the speed at which something is traveling through the air, but there is a different relationship depending on whether or not the speed is relatively low or high. Which of the following best gives the proportionalities for low and high speeds?
W) low speeds to v, high speeds to the square root of v
X) low speeds to square root of v, high speeds to v squared
Y) low speeds to v, high speeds to v squared
Z) low speeds to v squared, high speeds to v
ANSWER: Y) low speeds to v, high speeds to v squared
8. BONUS Physics Short Answer When an object is falling, eventually it reaches its terminal velocity. When this happens, it experiences no acceleration, because what two forces balance?
ANSWER: WEIGHT AND DRAG (also accept: GRAVITY AND AIR RESISTANCE; WEIGHT AND AIR RESISTANCE; GRAVITY AND DRAG)
9. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer Rank the following type of electromagnetic radiation in terms of increasing penetrating ability: radio, gamma, infrared, ultraviolet.
ANSWER: RADIO, INFRARED, ULTRAVIOLET, GAMMA
10. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice About how many times more energy does a high energy visible violet ray have than a low energy visible red ray?
W) 2 × 10-6
X) 2 × 10-3
Y) 2
Z) 2 × 103
ANSWER: Y) 2
11. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice The definition of a second is based upon vibrations of a cesium atom at which of the following temperatures?
W) 0 K
X) 100 K
Y) 273.15 K
Z) 373.25 K
ANSWER: W) 0 K
12. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Inside a hollow spherical mass, as you go from the center to the surface, there is
W) no gravitational force and no gravitational potential
X) changing gravitational force and constant gravitational potential
Y) no gravitational force and constant gravitational potential
Z) constant gravitational force and changing gravitational potential
ANSWER: Y) no gravitational force and constant gravitational potential
13. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Which of the following substances displays the biggest greenhouse effect in the Earth’s atmosphere?
W) carbon dioxide
X) methane
Y) water vapor
Z) dinitrogen tetroxide
ANSWER: Y) water vapor
14. BONUS Physics Short Answer The Earth radiates energy away primarily in what type of electromagnetic radiation?
ANSWER: INFRARED
15. BONUS Physics Short Answer Who invented one of the earliest accurate devices for measuring the speed of light, which uses a rotating gear, mirror, and light?
ANSWER: HIPPOLYTE FIZEAU (also accept: FIZEAU)
16. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Through radioactive decay, a carbon-11 atom turns into a boron-11 atom. This type of decay is
W) beta minus decay
X) beta plus decay
Y) alpha decay
Z) gamma decay
ANSWER: X) beta plus decay
17. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Harry is holding a wire in which electrons are flowing towards him. The magnetic field produced by the current, as seen by Harry, is
W) radially outward from the wire
X) radially inward towards the wire
Y) clockwise around the wire
Z) counterclockwise around the wire
ANSWER: Y) clockwise around the wire
18. BONUS Physics Short Answer The current through Harry’s wire is such that the magnetic field strength 5 centimeters away from the wire is 200 Teslas. What is the field strength 20 centimeters away from the wire?
ANSWER: 50 TESLA (also accept: 50 T; 50)
19. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice What is the distance between a node and antinode in a wave of wavelength L?
W) L/4
X) L/2
Y) L
Z) 2L
ANSWER: W) L/4
20. BONUS Physics Short Answer A standing second harmonic wave is generated on a string such that the two ends are held still. How many nodes and antinodes are there total along the length of the string?
ANSWER: 5
21. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Which of the following types of images do convex mirrors produce?
W) inverted, enlarged, and real
X) inverted, reduced, and virtual
Y) upright, enlarged, and real
Z) upright, reduced, and virtual
ANSWER: Z) upright, reduced, and virtual
22. BONUS Physics Short Answer An object is placed 60 centimeters from a convex mirror with focal length 30 centimeters. Where is the image formed?
ANSWER: 20 CENTIMETERS BEHIND MIRROR
23. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice The magnifying power of a telescope is equal to
W) the focal length of the objective divided by the focal length of the eyepiece
X) the focal length of the eyepiece divided by the focal length of the objective
Y) the focal length of the objective divided by the focal length of the secondary lens
Z) the focal length of the secondary lens divided by the focal length of the eyepiece
ANSWER: W) the focal length of the objective divided by the focal length of the eyepiece
24. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice At which of the following locations in a microscope would an Abbe condenser be placed?
W) below the light source
X) between the light source and the stage
Y) between the stage and the objective lens
Z) between the objective lens and the eyepiece
ANSWER: X) between the light source and the stage
25. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer Rank the de Broglie wavelengths of the following objects in increasing order by name: baseball moving at low speeds, electron moving at low speeds, electron moving at high speeds.
ANSWER: BASEBALL MOVING AT LOW SPEEDS, ELECTRON MOVING AT HIGH SPEEDS, ELECTRON MOVING AT LOW SPEEDS
26. BONUS Physics Short Answer What is the molar mass of heavy water?
ANSWER: 20 GRAMS PER MOLE (also accept: 20; 20 G/MOL)
27. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Which of the following is an example of secondary storage in most modern computers?
W) RAM
X) hard drives
Y) DRAM
Z) CDs
ANSWER: X) hard drives
28. BONUS Physics Short Answer Which of the following devices are considered solid-state? List them by name or number I) transistor II) vacuum tube III) diode IV) LCD V) hard drive.
ANSWER: I, III, AND IV (also accept: TRANSISTOR, DIODE, AND LCD; 1, 3, 4)
29. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice What is the baryon number of an up quark?
W) 0
X) 1/3
Y) 1/2
Z) 1
ANSWER: X) 1/3
30. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice The Paschen series dictates the energy produced if an electron in a hydrogen atom jumps from a higher energy level down to
W) n = 1
X) n = 2
Y) n = 3
Z) n = 4
ANSWER: Y) n = 3
31. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice A car that is moving twice as fast requires how many times more distance to stop, assuming the same braking force is applied?
W) 1
X) 2
Y) 4
Z) 8
ANSWER: Y) 4
32. BONUS Physics Short Answer List the seven basic units in the SI system.
ANSWER: KILOGRAM, METER, SECOND, AMPERE, KELVIN, CANDELA, MOLE
33. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice When light is reflected, it can be polarized to different amounts depending on the incoming angle and the refractive indexes. The angle for maximum polarization is known as
W) shielding angle
X) index angle
Y) Fresnel’s angle
Z) Brewster’s angle
ANSWER: Z) Brewster’s angle
34. BONUS Physics Short Answer What is Brewster’s angle for light in a vacuum that is incident upon a material with an index of refraction of the square root of 3?
ANSWER: 60° (also accept: 60 DEGREES; 60)
35. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice The direction of polarization of an electromagnetic ray is given based upon the direction of the
W) electric field vector
X) magnetic field vector
Y) velocity vector
Z) Poynting vector
ANSWER: W) electric field vector
36. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice When unpolarized light enters an ideal polarizer, the intensity of the light
W) remains the same
X) is reduced by a factor of the square root of 2
Y) is reduced by a factor of the square root of 3
Z) is reduced by a factor of 2
ANSWER: Z) is reduced by a factor of 2
37. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer What is the quantum for vibrational energy?
ANSWER: PHONON
38. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Which of the following is not a gauge boson?
W) photon
X) gluon
Y) muon
Z) Z boson
ANSWER: Y) muon
39. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice The type of stress induced by normal scissors is
W) tensile stress
X) bulk stress
Y) shear stress
Z) none of the above
ANSWER: Y) shear stress
40. BONUS Math Short Answer To three significant figures, what is the decimal value of eight billion, nine-hundred ninety four million, seven thousand fifty-three?
ANSWER: 8.99 × 109 (also accept: 8,990,000,000)
41. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer Every mass has a radius of a sphere that if compressed to within the radius, would turn into a black hole. What is this radius called?
ANSWER: SCHWARZSCHILD RADIUS
42. BONUS Physics Short Answer What is the name of the radiation black holes are predicted to emit, which lets them evaporate through mass loss?
ANSWER: HAWKING RADIATION
43. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice If light in one material is incident upon another material at the critical angle,
W) the light enters the second material along the normal to the border
X) the light is refracted as it enters the second material
Y) the light reflects at an angle back into the first material
Z) the light is refracted along the border between the surfaces
ANSWER: Z) the light is refracted along the border between the surfaces
44. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice In a concave spherical mirror, a real image is formed only if the object is
W) outside the focal point
X) at the focal point
Y) inside the focal point
Z) a concave spherical mirror always forms a real image
ANSWER: W) outside the focal point
45. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice An electromotive force of 12 volts is connected in series to an uncharged capacitor of capacitance 6 mF and a resistor of 2 Ω. After 12 milliseconds, the current flowing is
W) 6 A
X) 6/e A
Y) 3 A
Z) 3/e A
ANSWER: X) 6/e A
46. BONUS Physics Short Answer A particle with charge +1 mC is moving at 100 m/s through a field of 10 T that is angled 30° away. What is the force on the particle?
ANSWER: 0.5 NEWTONS (also accept: 0.5 N; 0.5)
47. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer Velocity is considered to be the derivative of position. What is the third derivative of position?
ANSWER: JERK
48. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice An object at rest experiences a jerk of 2 m/s3. What distance does the object travel in the first 9 seconds?
W) 81 meters
X) 243 meters
Y) 729 meters
Z) 27 meters
ANSWER: X) 243 meters
49. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer Single-beam gradient force traps are systems that use a highly focused laser beam to hold and move objects through a very small force that depends on the refractive mismatch. What is the common name for the system?
ANSWER: OPTICAL TWEEZERS (also accept: LASER TWEEZERS)
50. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Which principle do hydraulic drive systems work on?
W) Bernoulli’s Principle
X) Pascal’s Law
Y) Archimedes’ principle
Z) Newton’s 2nd Law
ANSWER: X) Pascal’s Law
51. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice In physics, CP violation attempts to explain
W) the existence of dark energy
X) why there is more matter than antimatter
Y) the expansion of the universe
Z) the function of the Higgs boson
ANSWER: X) why there is more matter than antimatter
52. BONUS Physics Short Answer What two quarks make up a pion+?
ANSWER: UP AND ANTI-DOWN (also accept: UP QUARK AND ANTIDOWN QUARK; UP AND ANTIDOWN)
53. BONUS Physics Short Answer BCS theory, which explains superconductivity in type-I superconductors, is named after the three physicists who developed it. Who are they?
ANSWER: BARDEEN, COOPER, AND SCHRIEFFER (also accept: BARDEEN, COOPER, SCHRIEFFER)
54. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice The most desirable material used to p-dope silicon for integrated circuits is
W) arsenic
X) phosphorous
Y) boron
Z) gallium
ANSWER: Y) boron
55. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer What are the two types of elementary fermions?
ANSWER: QUARKS AND LEPTONS
56. BONUS Physics Short Answer What are the two second-generation leptons (not including antiparticles)?
ANSWER: MUON AND MUON NEUTRINO (also accept: MUONS AND MUON NEUTRINOS)
57. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer A linear rod has a length of 100 meters. It experiences a temperature change of 2°C, and due to thermal expansion its length increases by 0.1 meter. What is the rod’s coefficient of linear thermal expansion?
ANSWER: 0.0005 PER °C (also accept: 1/2000; 5 × 10-4; 0.0005)
58. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice The temperature at which liquid water is most dense is closest to:
W) 0°C
X) 100°C
Y) 4°C
Z) 16°C
ANSWER: Y) 4°C
59. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice When two events occur at the same point in an inertial frame of reference, the time difference between the events in this frame is known as the
W) base time
X) proper time
Y) correct time
Z) origin time
ANSWER: X) proper time
60. BONUS Physics Short Answer Person A, who is not moving relative to an accurate clock, sees the clock tick for precisely five seconds. Person B is moving at √0.75 times the speed of light relative to person A. How much time passes in his inertial frame during those five seconds?
ANSWER: 10 SECONDS (also accept: 10)
61. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice A spring system oscillates. After each half-oscillation, the amplitude decreases. The spring system is experiencing
W) overdamping
X) critical damping
Y) underdamping
Z) simple harmonic motion
ANSWER: Y) underdamping
62. BONUS Physics Short Answer A bullet of mass 0.05 kg moving at 500 m/s collides inelastically with a wooden block of mass 4.95 kg, which is resting on a table with a coefficient of kinetic friction of 0.5. Assuming gravity is 10 m/s2, how long does it take for the bullet-wood system to come to a stop on the table?
ANSWER: 1 SECOND (also accept: 1)
63. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer What is the name for an artificially engineered material that has unusual physical properties like a negative index of refraction?
ANSWER: METAMATERIAL
64. BONUS Physics Short Answer The index of refraction of air, to 5 significant figures, is what?
ANSWER: 1.0003
65. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice A ray of light has a wavelength of 520 nm. Which of the rainbow colors is it considered?
W) red
X) yellow
Y) green
Z) blue
ANSWER: Y) green
66. BONUS Physics Short Answer An incandescent light bulb produces a continuous spectrum of light by heating a filament. What element is used for filaments in modern incandescents?
ANSWER: TUNGSTEN
67. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice If two systems are in thermal equilibrium with a third system, the two systems are also in thermal equilibrium with each other. This law is the
W) zeroth law of thermodynamics
X) first law of thermodynamics
Y) second law of thermodynamics
Z) third law of thermodynamics
ANSWER: W) zeroth law of thermodynamics
68. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice The second law of thermodynamics states which of the following:
W) entropy in a macroscopic system tends to decrease
X) entropy in a macroscopic system stays constant
Y) as the temperature of a system cools to absolute zero, entropy approaches a minimum
Z) entropy in a macroscopic system tends to increase
ANSWER: Z) entropy in a macroscopic system tends to increase
69. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Which of the following types of laser emits short bursts of rays, rather than a continuous beam?
W) ruby
X) Nd:YAG
Y) helium-neon
Z) carbon dioxide
ANSWER: W) ruby
70. BONUS Physics Short Answer This optical surface will only transmit light in a certain band of wavelengths, mostly reflecting all other light. What is it called?
ANSWER: DICHROIC MIRROR (also accept: DICHROIC FILTER; THIN-FILM FILTER; INTERFERENCE FILTER)
71. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice For a given non-superconducting wire, which of the following changes will decrease the resistance?
W) increase the cross-sectional area
X) increase the temperature
Y) increase the length
Z) increase the resistivity
ANSWER: W) increase the cross-sectional area
72. BONUS Physics Short Answer If the color stripes on a resistor read YELLOW BLUE GREEN GOLD, what is the resistance of the resistor and what is the precision of those color codes?
ANSWER: 4.6 × 106 OHMS, WITH A PRECISION OF 5% (also accept: 4.6 MEGAOHMS, 5%; 4.6 × 106 OHMS, 5%)
73. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice The word "physics" comes from a Greek word meaning
W) life
X) nature
Y) earth
Z) universe
ANSWER: X) nature
74. BONUS Physics Short Answer What is the name of the effect in which a magnet levitates above a superconductor?
ANSWER: MEISSNER EFFECT
75. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice A ray of light originating from a vacuum is incident upon a material whose index of refraction is √2 at an angle of 45° to the normal. By what angle is the ray refracted from its original path?
W) 5°
X) 15°
Y) 30°
Z) 45°
ANSWER: X) 15°
76. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice The momentum of a photon is equal to the energy of the photon divided by
W) its wavelength
X) Planck's constant
Y) its frequency
Z) the speed of light in a vacuum
ANSWER: Z) the speed of light in a vacuum
77. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer LASER is an acronym for what?
ANSWER: LIGHT AMPLIFICATION BY STIMULATED EMISSION OF RADIATION
78. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Lasers are classified into levels of safety. When pointed into an eye, a laser that is not powerful enough to cause damage before the reflexive blink is categorized as
W) class I
X) class II
Y) class IIIa
Z) class IIIb
ANSWER: X) class II
79. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer What principle states that no two fermions can occupy the same quantum state at the same time?
ANSWER: PAULI EXCLUSION PRINCIPLE
80. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Which of the following requires that fermions must have half-integer spins and bosons must have integer spins?
W) quantum-rotational theorem
X) Fermi-Bose spin theorem
Y) Einstein-angle theorem
Z) spin-statistics theorem
ANSWER: Z) spin-statistics theorem
81. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice When a real pulley is used in a system, which of the following is not a way in which the pulley will waste energy?
W) gravitational potential
X) rotational energy
Y) friction
Z) the pulley does not waste any energy
ANSWER: W) gravitational potential
82. BONUS Physics Short Answer A frictionless pulley is attached to a 6 kg mass on one side and a 4 kg mass on the other. Assuming gravity is 10 m/s2, what is the magnitude of the acceleration experienced by each mass?
ANSWER: 2 M/S2 (also accept: 2)
83. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer What law dictates the direction of current flow in electromagnetic induction?
ANSWER: LENZ'S LAW (also accept: LENZ)
84. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Kerr black holes possess
W) mass and angular momentum
X) mass and charge
Y) mass, charge, and angular momentum
Z) mass only
ANSWER: W) mass and angular momentum
85. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer How many angstroms are in one gigameter?
ANSWER: 1 × 1019 (also accept: 1019)
86. BONUS Physics Short Answer In physics, one frequently refers to SI as a system with set units of measurement. What is SI an abbreviation for? (You can give either the French name or the English translation.)
ANSWER: INTERNATIONAL SYSTEM OF UNITS (also accept: LE SYSTÈME INTERNATIONAL D'UNITÉS)
87. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice In a convex mirror, the image produced is always?
W) virtual and inverted
X) real and inverted
Y) virtual and erect
Z) real and erect
ANSWER: Y) virtual and erect
88. BONUS Physics Short Answer In a geometric diagram of light reflecting off a mirror, what are the four principal rays?
ANSWER: PARALLEL TO AXIS, THROUGH FOCAL POINT, THROUGH CENTER OF CURVATURE, TO THE VERTEX
89. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer Two cars are 100 meters apart. Car A is moving towards car B at 20 meters per second, and car B is moving towards car A at 5 meters per second. When the cars finally meet, the cars collide inelastically. Assuming they both are 1000 kg, what is their speed after the collision?
ANSWER: 7.5 METERS PER SECOND (also accept: 7.5)
90. BONUS Physics Short Answer A 0.05-gram dead mosquito is lying on the ground when a bird comes to eat it. The bird flies the mosquito up 100 meters and then drops it. The mosquito falls freely, reaching a terminal velocity of 0.4 m/s. At impact, the mosquito is stopped over a distance of 0.5 mm. What average force is applied to the mosquito by the ground?
ANSWER: 0.008 NEWTONS (also accept: 0.008 N; 0.008)
91. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Gluons are bosons that are the force carriers of which fundamental force?
W) gravitational force
X) electromagnetic force
Y) strong nuclear force
Z) weak nuclear force
ANSWER: Y) strong nuclear force
92. BONUS Physics Short Answer What two bosons are associated with the weak nuclear force?
ANSWER: W AND Z BOSONS (also accept: W AND Z)
93. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Fermions have
W) quarter-integer spins
X) half-integer spins
Y) integer spins
Z) no spin
ANSWER: X) half-integer spins
94. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice If a composite particle is composed of 4 fermions and 3 bosons, it is a
W) fermion
X) boson
Y) lepton
Z) quark
ANSWER: X) boson
95. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Bosons are force-carriers of fundamental forces. Which of the fundamental forces has a boson not been experimentally found for?
W) electromagnetic force
X) gravitational force
Y) strong nuclear force
Z) weak nuclear force
ANSWER: X) gravitational force
96. BONUS Physics Short Answer Bosons are particles that obey B-E statistics. What two names make up the initials B and E?
ANSWER: BOSE AND EINSTEIN
97. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice A 3 kilogram object is dropped from a height of 1 km on the planet Najeun, where the gravity is 1/2 that of Earth’s. (Assume Earth’s gravity to be 10 m/s2.) How many seconds later will the object hit the ground?
W) 15 s
X) 20 s
Y) 25 s
Z) 30 s
ANSWER: X) 20 s
98. BONUS Physics Short Answer A 2 kg object moving at 3 m/s has an inelastic collision with a 3 kg object at rest. What is the new speed of the combined objects?
ANSWER: 1.2 M/S (also accept: 6/5 M/S; 1.2)
99. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice What is the charge of a Z boson, in multiples of the elementary charge?
W) 0
X) 1
Y) -1
Z) 2
ANSWER: W) 0
100. BONUS Physics Short Answer What is the antiparticle of the Z boson?
ANSWER: ITSELF (also accept: Z BOSON; THE Z BOSON)
101. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice If an atom emits an alpha particle, its atomic mass number and charge change by
W) -1 and -2
X) -2 and -2
Y) -2 and 0
Z) -4 and -2
ANSWER: Z) -4 and -2
102. BONUS Physics Short Answer What is the atomic number of an atom of uranium-238 if it emits an alpha particle?
ANSWER: 90
103. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Which of the following scientists did not discover the electron, neutron, or proton?
W) Rutherford
X) Chadwick
Y) Thomson
Z) Yukawa
ANSWER: Z) Yukawa
104. BONUS Physics Short Answer A 50 kg man climbs up Half Dome from the floor of Yosemite Valley, ascending 1500 meters. To two significant figures, how many kilocalories does he convert into gravitational potential energy? Take g to be 10 meters per second squared.
ANSWER: 180 KILOCALORIES (also accept: 180)
105. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice At the beginning of a proton-proton chain reaction, deuterium is produced. What is the ratio of protons to neutrons in deuteron?
W) 1:2
X) 2:1
Y) 1:1
Z) 2:3
ANSWER: Y) 1:1
106. BONUS Physics Short Answer When two hydrogen-1 atoms combine to form a deuterium atom, what particles do they release?
ANSWER: POSITRON AND ELECTRON NEUTRINO (also accept: ANTIELECTRON AND ELECTRON NEUTRINO; POSITRON AND NEUTRINO)
107. BONUS Physics Short Answer The law that energy is always conserved is which law of thermodynamics?
ANSWER: FIRST LAW (also accept: 1ST LAW OF THERMODYNAMICS; FIRST LAW OF THERMODYNAMICS)
108. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice A 3 kilogram object is dropped from a height of 1 km on the airless planet Calebon, where the gravitational acceleration is 1/2 that of Earth’s. How many seconds later will the object hit the ground?
W) 15
X) 20
Y) 25
Z) 30
ANSWER: X) 20
109. BONUS Physics Short Answer A 2 kg object moving at 3 m/s undergoes an inelastic collision with a 3 kg object that is initially at rest. If they sit on a horizontal surface with a coefficient of friction of 0.2, how far will they slide before they come to rest?
ANSWER: 0.36 METERS (also accept: 0.36; 0.37)
110. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice What term describes that the wavelength of a particle is equivalent to the wavelength of a photon whose energy is the same as the rest mass energy of the particle?
W) rest wavelength
X) de Broglie wavelength
Y) Einstein wavelength
Z) Compton wavelength
ANSWER: Z) Compton wavelength
111. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice In inelastic Compton scattering,
W) the total mass-energy and the total momentum are conserved
X) the total mass-energy is conserved but the total momentum is not conserved
Y) the total mass-energy is not conserved but the total momentum is conserved
Z) neither the total mass-energy nor the total momentum are conserved
ANSWER: W) the total mass-energy and the total momentum are conserved
112. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice When an object is moving through a fluid, the Reynolds number
W) increases with increasing viscosity of the fluid
X) stays constant with increasing viscosity of the fluid
Y) decreases with increasing viscosity of the fluid
Z) depends on the viscosity’s relation to a threshold value
ANSWER: Y) decreases with increasing viscosity of the fluid
113. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Stokes' law defines the drag force when a small spherical object is moving with a small Reynolds number in a continuous viscous fluid. It says that the magnitude of the drag force is approximately equal to the fluid viscosity times the sphere's radius times the velocity at which the particles are moving times a constant. When the three variables are in SI units, this constant is
W) 4π2
X) √(2π)
Y) ln π
Z) 6π
ANSWER: Z) 6π
114. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice How many elementary leptons, including corresponding antiparticles, exist?
W) 3
X) 6
Y) 12
Z) 36
ANSWER: Y) 12
115. BONUS Physics Short Answer Not including antiparticles, what two particles make up the third generation leptons?
ANSWER: TAU AND TAU NEUTRINO (also accept: TAU LEPTON AND TAU NEUTRINO)
116. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer What law dictates the magnetic field produced by current?
ANSWER: AMPÈRE'S LAW (also accept: AMPERE'S LAW; BIOT-SAVART LAW)
117. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice A wire is pointing straight towards you, with current flowing towards you as well. The magnetic field produced is
W) pointing radially outward from the wire
X) pointing radially inward from the wire
Y) circling the wire, clockwise
Z) circling the wire, counterclockwise
ANSWER: Z) circling the wire, counterclockwise
118. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer The splitting of absorption lines due to the presence of an electric field is known as the what?
ANSWER: INVERSE STARK EFFECT (also accept: STARK EFFECT)
120. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice A positive point charge is stationary. Electrical equipotential lines caused by this charge will be
W) radial lines pointing outward
X) radial lines pointing inward
Y) concentric circles
Z) there are no equipotential lines
ANSWER: Y) concentric circles

122. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice When light is incident upon a material of a different index of refraction, some of the incident light is reflected and some is refracted. As the angle of the incident light from the normal increases from 0° to 90°, which is true?

Y) more of the light is polarized
Z) more of the light is phase shifted
ANSWER: W) more of the light is reflected

X) the water will appear polarized while the mirage will appear unpolarized

Z) both sources are polarized-Mr. Maine is stuck anyway.
ANSWER: X) the water will appear polarized while the mirage will appear unpolarized

X) critical damping

360. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer This experiment consisted of steadily increasing voltage on a tube filled with low density mercury vapor and observing the current that successfully passed from the cathode to the anode. The experiment showed that at very precise intervals the current drops off, and it was used as evidence to back up the Bohr model of the atom, and earned a Nobel prize for two people in 1925. What is this experiment called?
ANSWER: FRANCK-HERTZ EXPERIMENT (also accept: FRANCK HERTZ; FRANCK-HERTZ)

Y) at any given height, pressure of a pipe is constant

464. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer List which, if any, of the following are ferromagnetic at STP: aluminum, cobalt, silver, chromium, gadolinium.
ANSWER: COBALT AND GADOLINIUM
465. BONUS Physics Short Answer What is the name of the state in a ferromagnetic material at which all domains are oriented in the same way, such that no greater magnetization can occur?
ANSWER: SATURATION (also accept: SATURATION POINT; MAGNETIC SATURATION)
466. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer When an object spins in a fluid medium, the object is pushed in a direction perpendicular to its motion, which is responsible for the curving of spinning balls. What is this effect known as?
ANSWER: MAGNUS EFFECT
467. BONUS Physics Short Answer In Rayleigh scattering, photons are elastically scattered. This can be contrasted with what type of scattering, in which photons scatter inelastically?
ANSWER: RAMAN SCATTERING (also accept: RAMAN; COMPTON SCATTERING)
468. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Harry has a pair of pretty awesome laser-protection sunglasses. They are reddish in color, so they protect best against which of the following wavelength ranges?
W) 300-400 nanometers

ANSWER: X) 450-550 nanometers

ANSWER: INFRARED

ANSWER: SUPERFLUID

ANSWER: HANS DEHMELT (also accept: DEHMELT; HANS GEORG DEHMELT)

X) Faraday’s law

ANSWER: Y) Biot-Savart law
473. BONUS Physics Short Answer Two infinite parallel wires are separated by a distance of 1 meter. They each carry the same current in the same direction, and feel an attractive force of 2 × 10-7 newtons per meter of length. What current is running through these wires?
ANSWER: 1 AMPERE (also accept: 1; 1 A)
474. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice When referring to magnetism, which of the following is true?
W) Relative permeability is synonymous with susceptibility.
X) The susceptibility of a diamagnet is less than zero.
Y) Susceptibility is always one greater than relative permeability.
Z) Relative permeability of a paramagnet is less than one.
ANSWER: X) The susceptibility of a diamagnet is less than zero.
475. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Curie’s law indicates that
W) diamagnetism increases with decreasing temperature
X) paramagnetism decreases with increasing temperature
Y) ferromagnetism turns into diamagnetism above a certain temperature
Z) magnetic domains grow in size as temperature increases
ANSWER: X) paramagnetism decreases with increasing temperature

Z) Hertz
ANSWER: Z) Hertz
477. BONUS Physics Short Answer An electromagnetic wave has a wavelength of 15 centimeters in a vacuum. What is its frequency in Hertz?
ANSWER: 2 × 109 HERTZ (also accept: 2 GIGAHERTZ; 2 GHZ)
478. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice In a linear electromagnetic wave,
W) electric field is in phase with magnetic field
X) electric field leads magnetic field by 90°
Y) electric field lags magnetic field by 90°
Z) electric field is 180° out of phase with magnetic field
ANSWER: W) electric field is in phase with magnetic field

W) c = E * B
X) E = c * B
Y) B = c * E
Z) c = (E * B)2
ANSWER: X) E = c * B

ANSWER: X) electric field is out of phase with magnetic field by 90°

484. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice A thermal neutron is best described as
W) an extremely cool neutron that moves at speeds much slower than the molecules around it

703. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer If light travels from a crystal in which the index of refraction is 2.2 into a liquid in which the index of refraction is 1.1, what is the critical angle in degrees?
ANSWER: 30 DEGREES (also accept: 30; 30°)

ANSWER: Y) 450 nm light

ANSWER: h/4π (also accept: ħ/2; H-BAR OVER 2; H OVER 4 PI)
706. BONUS Physics Short Answer This electromagnetic phenomenon occurs when an increasing magnetic field intersecting a conductor induces circles of current within the conductor. What is it?
ANSWER: EDDY CURRENTS (also accept: EDDY CURRENT)

ANSWER: FERMAT'S PRINCIPLE

ANSWER: ELECTRON ANTINEUTRINO
710. BONUS Physics Short Answer This is a mechanism for converting the linear motion of a slider into rotational motion or vice-versa. It is most commonly used coupled with the reciprocating motion of a piston and is preferred to a crankshaft and connecting rod setup because it has fewer moving parts and smoother operation. What is it?
ANSWER: SCOTCH YOKE

713. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice The velocities of linear mechanical waves depend only on:
W) the medium through which the waves travel
X) the initial force that creates the waves
Y) the amplitude of the waves
Z) the frequency of the waves
ANSWER: W) the medium through which the waves travel
714. BONUS Physics Short Answer A transverse wave is created when a pulse is sent down a stretched rope in which the tension is 50 N. If the rope's mass of 1 kg is distributed uniformly throughout its 0.5 meter length, what is the speed of the wave?
ANSWER: 5 METERS PER SECOND (also accept: 5)
715. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer What is the term for the concept that energy can exist only in discrete increments, such as photons?
ANSWER: QUANTIZATION (also accept: QUANTUM THEORY)

ANSWER: PSI

ANSWER: ISAAC NEWTON (also accept: NEWTON)
718. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice The thrust of a rocket is equal to:
W) the square root of the exhaust velocity
X) the exhaust velocity multiplied by the instantaneous change in mass per unit time
Y) the exhaust velocity divided by the instantaneous change in mass per unit time
Z) the exhaust velocity multiplied by the instantaneous change in momentum per unit time
ANSWER: X) the exhaust velocity multiplied by the instantaneous change in mass per unit time
719. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer This is a circuit that can measure an unknown emf by comparison with a known emf using a variable resistor. What is it?
ANSWER: POTENTIOMETER

W) glide potentiometer
X) Wheatstone potentiometer
Y) ruler potentiometer
Z) slide-wire potentiometer
ANSWER: Z) slide-wire potentiometer
721. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice A transverse wave travels along a stretched string towards the fixed end of the string. When the wave reaches the fixed end, it:
W) reflects, traveling in the opposite direction without being inverted
X) reflects, traveling in the opposite direction and inverted
Y) reflects, traveling in the opposite direction at much lower speed
Z) transfers its energy to the string in the form of heat
ANSWER: X) reflects, traveling in the opposite direction and inverted
722. BONUS Physics Short Answer Longitudinal waves with frequencies below about 20 Hz are called what?
ANSWER: INFRASONIC (also accept: INFRASOUND)

X) Bremsstrahlung

ANSWER: X) Bremsstrahlung

730. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice To create a n-type semiconductor, another substance will be added to the original semimetal in a process called doping. In a n-type semiconductor, what is the relationship between an atom of the semimetal and an atom of the dopant?
W) the dopant atoms are more numerous than the semiconductor atoms
X) the dopant is isoelectronic with the semimetal
Y) the dopant has more valence electrons than the semimetal
Z) the dopant has fewer valence electrons than the semimetal
ANSWER: Y) the dopant has more valence electrons than the semimetal

733. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Ferromagnetic material does not smoothly align itself in the direction of a field, but instead small portions progressively align their individual magnetic dipoles with the field. This is called the:
W) Curie effect
X) Barkhausen effect
Y) Neel effect
Z) Gauss effect
ANSWER: X) Barkhausen effect

821. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Total mechanical energy in a system is conserved only if the forces doing work in or on the system are:
W) zero
X) nonconservative
Y) constant
Z) conservative
ANSWER: Z) conservative
822. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice The force responsible for keeping the nucleus stable is:
W) gravitational
X) electromagnetic
Y) strong
Z) weak
ANSWER: Y) strong
823. BONUS Physics Short Answer Of the four fundamental forces, which two are known to be two different aspects of the same force, and what is this force called?
ANSWER: ELECTROMAGNETIC AND WEAK; ELECTROWEAK (also accept: ELECTROMAGNETISM AND THE WEAK FORCE; THE ELECTROWEAK FORCE)
824. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice The resistive force on an oscillating object is small compared to the restoring force, so that the oscillatory character of the motion is preserved but the amplitude of vibration decreases with time. This system is said to be:
W) overdamped
X) hyperdamped
Y) underdamped
Z) hypodamped
ANSWER: Y) underdamped
825. BONUS Physics Short Answer If the resistive force on an otherwise oscillatory object is such that it does not oscillate, but is instead returned directly to equilibrium via exponential decay, the system is said to be what?
ANSWER: CRITICALLY DAMPED (also accept: OVERDAMPED)
826. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer What is the term for the individual light and dark lines in a diffraction pattern?
ANSWER: FRINGES

X) rarefactions
Y) displacement antinodes
Z) expansion regions
ANSWER: X) rarefactions
828. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer A pendulum that swings with simple harmonic motion but cannot reasonably be treated as a point mass is given what name?
ANSWER: PHYSICAL PENDULUM

W) inverted but not reflected

ANSWER: Y) inverted and reflected

Z) avalanche diode
ANSWER: X) step-recovery diode

W) the transfer of heat through the superfluid
X) the propagation of compression waves through the superfluid
Y) the time derivative of the second sound of heat transfer
Z) the propagation of waves through the Rollin film
ANSWER: Z) the propagation of waves through the Rollin film
1044. BONUS Physics Short Answer In a superfluid, a Rollin film will form to allow the superfluid to creep up a surface and enter a container to even out the levels of fluid. What is this effect, named after the first person to create liquid helium and observe superconductivity, called?
ANSWER: ONNES EFFECT

W) radiant flux

Y) illuminance

ANSWER: Y) illuminance
1047. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice The rings through which particles travel in a synchrotron are designed to be absolutely gigantic primarily because
W) the larger the ring, the more easily heat energy can be dissipated.
X) the larger the ring, the less likely an accidental collision will occur.
Y) the smaller the ring, the larger the acceleration the particles undergo is.
Z) the smaller the ring, the less energy can be delivered to the particles during one revolution.
ANSWER: Y) the smaller the ring, the larger the acceleration the particles undergo is.
1048. BONUS Physics Short Answer An electron of mass 9.1 × 10-31 kg and charge -1.6 × 10-19 C is accelerated in the path of a ring. According to classical physics, what is the frequency of the electromagnetic radiation that is emitted by the electron?

1155. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Which of the following is NOT true about spectral forbidden lines?
W) They typically do not occur if other processes, such as collisional de-excitation, are available to return excited atoms to their ground states
X) If no other processes are available to return excited atoms to their ground states, then the atom is never able to cross the forbidden line
Y) Forbidden lines are most prominent in low density gases due to the low probability of collisions
Z) Forbidden lines are formed by metastable states that take hundreds of thousands of times longer to decay than normal spontaneous emission
ANSWER: X) If no other processes are available to return excited atoms to their ground states, then the atom is never able to cross the forbidden line

1414. BONUS Physics Short Answer Two wheels with moments of inertia I1 and I2 respectively are rotating on a pole with angular speeds of ω1 and ω2. An external force pushes both of the wheels together with a force that creates no torque. After a while, what is the new angular speed of both wheels?
ANSWER: (I1 ω1 + I2 ω2)/(I1 + I2)

1619. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice How is the energy E of a mechanical wave related to its amplitude A?
W) E is proportional to A
X) E is proportional to A squared
Y) E is proportional to 1/A
Z) E is proportional to 1/A squared
ANSWER: X) E is proportional to A squared

W) E is proportional to f
X) E is proportional to f squared
Y) E is proportional to 1/f
Z) E is proportional to 1/f squared
ANSWER: X) E is proportional to f squared

X) the coefficient of kinetic friction is usually greater than static

Z) once the object is moving both coefficients become zero

1669. BONUS Physics Short Answer From the following, give by number the correct statement or statements that are true about a spring that is underdamped. 1) The system oscillates at its natural resonant frequency. 2) The damping ratio of the system is equal to 0. 3) The system oscillates with the amplitude gradually decreasing to zero. 4) The damping ratio of the system is bigger than 0 but smaller than 1.

W) Changing current
X) Oscillating magnetic dipole
Y) Steady current
Z) Changing electric flux
ANSWER: Y) Steady current
1735. BONUS Physics Short Answer Which of the following cases could produce a stable equilibrium in classical electromagnetism? I. Four negative charges surrounding a positive charge II. A positive charge and a negative charge in close vicinity III. Five positive charges located in the shape of an arc IV. Five negative charges in a circular orbit around a positive charge of equal size
ANSWER: NONE
1736. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Whereas the study of moving fluids is called fluid dynamics, the study of the flow of electrically conducting fluids in electromagnetic fields is called:
W) Electrodynamics
X) Electrostatics
Y) Magnetostatics
Z) Magnetohydrodynamics
ANSWER: Z) Magnetohydrodynamics
1737. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice What conditions must be met for a physical dipole to tend toward a perfect one?
W) Lower distance between charges, increase charge
X) Lower distance between charges, decrease charge
Y) Higher distance between charges, increase charge
Z) Higher distance between charges, decrease charge
ANSWER: W) Lower distance between charges, increase charge
1738. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Which is the name of the temperature at which ferromagnetic metals lose their ferromagnetic properties?
W) Flux temperature
X) Critical temperature
Y) Curie point
Z) Bohr Point
ANSWER: Y) Curie point
1739. BONUS Physics Short Answer What curve represents the magnetization of a permanent magnet vs. the current which is generating the magnetic field?
ANSWER: HYSTERESIS LOOP (also accept: HYSTERESIS CURVE)
1740. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice What is the name for the speed in which a gas will start to ionize and will not increase or decrease its speed until it is almost fully ionized?
W) Ion escape speed
X) Critical ionization velocity
Y) Ion current
Z) Delocalized ion heating
ANSWER: X) Critical ionization velocity
1741. BONUS Physics Short Answer A neutral gas is composed entirely of atoms with average mass m and an ionization potential energy of U. What is the critical ionization velocity of the gas?
ANSWER: √(2U/m) (also accept: THE SQUARE ROOT OF 2U/M)
1742. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice A plasma containing nanometer or micrometer sized particles is called a:
W) Brownian plasma
X) Reduced plasma
Y) Dusty plasma
Z) Hot plasma
ANSWER: Y) Dusty plasma
1743. BONUS Physics Short Answer In the single-fluid theory of plasmas, one can treat a plasma as governed by which (if any) of the following equations I. Maxwell’s equations II. Navier-Stokes equations III. Newton’s equations IV. Boltzmann’s equations
ANSWER: I AND II (also accept: MAXWELL'S EQUATIONS AND THE NAVIER-STOKES EQUATIONS)

W) Crookes
X) Debye
Y) Langmuir
Z) Alfven

1745. BONUS Physics Short Answer What is the region of the Earth’s magnetosphere consisting of low energy plasma?

1747. BONUS Physics Short Answer What is the term defined as the maximum amount of electric current a conductor or device can carry before sustaining any type of deterioration?

X) 50 Watts

Z) 500 Watts
ANSWER: Z) 500 Watts
1751. BONUS Physics Short Answer Which theorem states that in a perfectly conducting fluid, the magnetic flux through any closed loop moving with the fluid is constant in time?

X) Magnetic
Y) Chemical
Z) Gravitational

1753. BONUS Physics Short Answer What formulas relate the displacement and auxiliary fields to the electric and magnetic fields respectively in linear media?

1754. BONUS Physics Short Answer An electron enters into a magnetic field and begins to experience helical motion before changing direction in another magnetic field pointing in a new direction. Let R be the radius of the first helix and Z be the radius of the second helix. Assuming the charge of the electron is e and its mass is m, what is the total power dissipated by the magnetic fields as the electron travels through them?
ANSWER: 0 (also accept: ZERO)
1755. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer What phenomenon refers to momentum transfer due to the absorption or reflection of a photon?

1759. BONUS Physics Short Answer Which precise mathematical statement of local conservation relates the flow of charge to the rate at which the charge is changing?

1765. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer What is the term for when a fluid flows in parallel layers, with no disruption between the layers?

1768. BONUS Physics Short Answer Whenever the speed of a wave depends on its frequency, the supporting medium is called what?

1772. BONUS Physics Short Answer A very long solenoid has a current running through it. Coaxial with the solenoid are two long cylindrical shells, one inside and one outside and each of opposite charge. If the current is gradually reduced, the cylinders begin to rotate. Where does the angular momentum come from?
ANSWER: THE ELECTROMAGNETIC FIELD (also accept: THE ELECTRIC AND MAGNETIC FIELDS; THE FIELDS)

1775. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Under the Gilbert model of magnetism, magnetic dipoles are composed of what?

X) A north monopole and a south monopole

1800. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Which of the following is NOT true?
W) Kepler's second law directly correlates the square of a planet's semi-major axis to the cube of its period
X) The rotational kinetic energy of a disk is proportional to the square of its angular speed
Y) Osmosis is the diffusion of water across a semi-permeable membrane from a high water concentration to a low water concentration
Z) Two objects at thermal equilibrium with each other are at the same temperature
ANSWER: W) Kepler's second law directly correlates the square of a planet's semi-major axis to the cube of its period

W) 60°
X) 45°

1802. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Which of the following is true of the relationship between current and voltage across capacitors in AC circuits?
W) Voltage leads Current by 90°.
X) Voltage and Current are in phase.
Y) Current leads Voltage by 90°.
Z) Voltage and Current are 180° out of phase.
ANSWER: Y) Current leads Voltage by 90°.

Z) Anderson experiment

1805. TOSS-UP Physics Short Answer Light, with a frequency f, is incident on a photoemissive metal. It is observed that electrons are emitted with both minimal and maximum kinetic energies. By name or number, identify all of the following four actions that can be done to increase the maximum kinetic energy of the emitted electrons. I. Increase the intensity of the incident light II. Increase the frequency of the incident light III. Decrease the frequency of the incident light IV. Decrease the work function by using a different metal
ANSWER: II AND IV (also accept: 2 AND 4)
1806. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice Two parallel slits are 1.0 × 10-3 meters apart. The light passing through them has a wavelength of 5.4 × 10-7 meters. It falls on a viewing screen 2.0 meters away from the slits. Approximately what is the distance, in meters, between the central bright band and the first band next to it?

Z) 3.6 × 10-3
ANSWER: W) 1.1 × 10-3

1808. BONUS Physics Multiple Choice An electromotive force of 0.003 volts is induced in a wire when it moves at right angles to a uniform magnetic field with a speed of 4.0 meters per second. If the length of wire in the field is 15 centimeters, what is the flux density, in teslas?

1811. TOSS-UP Physics Multiple Choice Norton’s Theorem states that any complicated circuit of resistors and voltages may be rearranged into
W) an equivalent resistance in series with an equivalent voltage.
X) an equivalent resistance in parallel with an equivalent voltage.
Y) an equivalent resistance in series with an equivalent current source.
Z) an equivalent resistance in parallel with an equivalent current source.
ANSWER: Z) an equivalent resistance in parallel with an equivalent current source.
